HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B002","root_000745/B005","root_000745/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_07c181937fca1e8297f6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:18:2\",\"root_000745\",[\"B005\",\"B008\"]],[\"88:18:4\",\"root_000582\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gök ile açılıştaki ad arasındaki ortak yükselme çağrışımı, ilahi adın yüceliğini kozmik düzenin görünür yüksekliğiyle yankılandırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B002","root_000745/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_10c0efedf6fa8cbcebea","text":"2. **Name/elevation echo from the basmalah.** Evidence: `س م و` B005 name as raised designation in 88:0; B008 good repute; `ر ف ع` B002 high rank, B005 announcing a report. Structural cue: the same root appears in the opening `بسم` and here as `سماء`. Role: sky elevation reflects the raised designation that opened the surah. Abductive move: vertical order and named authority are mutually illuminating. Reading change: the ayah reads as cosmic enactment of the opening name’s elevation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000582/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_37b807a8dec8da2491cf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:18:4\",\"root_000582\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Edilgen yükseltiliş ve sonradan insan denetiminin reddi, kozmik yapının insan egemenliğini aşan bir kurucu iradeye işaret etmesini sağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4bcb7dfd59d08e7ec53f","text":"The basmalah root link was the key surprise: `بسم` and `سماء` share `س م و`, making named invocation and raised sky part of one elevation field. The later denial of `مسيطر` made the passive cosmic raising important for agency.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5c9810baeb8da7af91a8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:18:4\",\"root_000582\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yükseltme aynı zamanda sunma işlevi görerek fiziksel uzaklığına rağmen göğü bakışın önüne getirir; burada yükseklik nesneyi gizlemek yerine dikkate açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_60bff300bffe4e2b163a","text":"88:19-20 completed the architectural relation among cover, upright mass, and floor. More unexpectedly, 88:21 made the raised sky function like a report that can be verbalized, and 88:23's turning away showed that this visible announcement can be refused.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6d702c7570fc91736ae2","text":"3. **Raisedness as non-human control.** Evidence: `ر ف ع` B001/B002; later `س طر` B003 controlling authority is denied to the messenger; `ء ل ه` B002 divine name. Structural cue: the sky is passive `رفعت`, and the messenger later is not controller. Role: the raised sky witnesses an agency beyond human domination. Abductive move: passive cosmic formation points to divine action without requiring the reminder to coerce. Reading change: `رفعت` reads as a sign of agency placed above human control.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_95e85ab726ea1b0fb323","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:18:4\",\"root_000582\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yükseltme bir haberi ilan eder gibi, göğü sözlü hatırlatmadan önce herkesin önüne asılmış okunabilir bir bildiriye dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000745/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a75f55c6fdadfd6b1324","text":"2. **Elevation makes the overhead cover a visible, looming outline.** **Lexical:** `س م و` B002, \"looming raised outline,\" alongside B004; `ر ف ع` B001 supplies the raising operation. **Cue:** `إِلَى` directs the observer upward to a form whose height lets it remain visually present over the whole field. **Role:** B002 gives visibility to elevation, while B004 gives the visible form its covering function. **Abduction:** I infer that being raised enables comprehensive visibility; the packet names looking and height but not optical causation. **Reading change:** the sky is not empty distance but a raised outline that both covers and presents itself for inspection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000745/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b12584be50ffb7a1449b","text":"1. **Sky as overhead covering raised.** Evidence: `س م و` B004 overhead sky/cover, B001 rising high; `ر ف ع` B001 raising upward. `ك ي ف` has no accepted branch inventory in the packet. Structural cue: the sky follows the camel and precedes mountains/earth in the inspection chain. Role: the sky is the upper cover, raised rather than crushing. Abductive move: a covering can be merciful architecture rather than `غاشية` punishment. Reading change: the raised sky becomes the benign counterpart to the overwhelming cover.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B009","root_000582/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b86f2950c8fd25dce47c","text":"3. **The sky's elevation acts as a public report before the verbal reminder.** **Lexical:** `ر ف ع` B005, \"announcing a report,\" activated alongside B001; `ذ ك ر` B009 in 88:21 will name reminder. **Cue:** visible constructions in 88:17-20 are followed by `فَذَكِّرْ`, and turning away appears in 88:23. **Role:** B005 lets the raised sky function as publicly displayed information; the reminder verbalizes what elevation has already made manifest. **Abduction:** I infer that visibility communicates and that turning away refuses that communication. **Reading change:** `رُفِعَتْ` can be heard as both construction and announcement: the sky is raised into a legible sign.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000745/B002","root_000745/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c0d12ecd2b8561d5bed0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:18:2\",\"root_000745\",[\"B002\",\"B004\"]],[\"88:18:4\",\"root_000582\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gök, boş uzaklık değil; yükseltilerek bütün görüş alanının üstünde belirgin ve incelenebilir hale gelen bir örtü çizgisidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c7bde277b4cf5e60c52e","text":"4. **Raising also presents the object to attention rather than carrying it away.** **Lexical:** `ر ف ع` B004, \"bringing near or presenting.\" **Cue:** despite physical remoteness, `وَإِلَى` makes the sky an immediate grammatical object of directed sight. **Role:** the presentation branch resolves the tension between height and cognitive accessibility. **Abduction:** I infer perceptual nearness from syntactic direction, not physical distance. **Reading change:** elevation is a mode of presentation: the farther object is made available to insight precisely by being set high before the gaze.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000745/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e025378808b26ac66671","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:18:2\",\"root_000745\",[\"B004\"]],[\"88:18:4\",\"root_000582\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yükseltilmiş gök, sedirin dünya ölçeğindeki karşılığı ve kuşatıcı felaketin ezmeyen, yararlı örtüye çevrilmiş karşıtıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000745/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f9639862914ca70a04fb","text":"1. **The raised sky is the macro-scale counterpart of the raised couch.** **Lexical:** `س م و` B004, \"overhead sky and cover\"; `ر ف ع` B001, physical raising. **Cue:** the identical `ر ف ع` root describes couches in 88:13 and the sky in 88:18, while repeated `كَيْفَ` places the latter inside an observational series. **Role:** the couch supplies supported human-scale elevation; the sky supplies covering world-scale elevation. **Abduction:** I infer constructional analogy from root recurrence at the two ends of the five-ayah span. **Reading change:** the sky is read as usable architecture writ large, and the earlier interior as a local echo of cosmic arrangement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
